                with the canonical-key '|' separator collapsed to
                '_' for analyst URL clarity.
              derived.signal_donor_on_leie Dagster asset
                -- declares deps on raw.fec_contribution and
                raw.hhs_oig_leie ONLY. The shared L1 table is
                sliced by (cycle, signal_id), so concurrent
                refreshers writing disjoint slices are safe.
              derived.v_entity_fraud_risk gains
                signal_donor_on_leie as a sixth upstream dep.
              Asset check match_rate_in_plausible_range (WARN):
                Standard cross-source-signal semantics (NOT the
                inverted semantics of entity_funded_and_excluded
                where zero is healthy). Zero matches when both
                substrates are non-empty indicates a
                canonicalizer or join-column regression; rate >1%
                indicates over-matching.
                Different bound from entity_funded_and_excluded
                because the donor population is orders of magnitude
                larger than the federal-contractor population, so
                the LEIE-overlap is small but non-zero in steady
                state -- a few dozen to a few hundred matched
                donors per cycle is the realistic expected band.
            Deliberately out of scope (documented in migration 059
            header for future-session traceability):
              * Candidate-side projection ("which candidates
                received money from LEIE-excluded donors?"). Same
                shape as 057, deferred so the L1 substrate ships
                clean first.
              * State / DOB filtering (same v1 stance as the
                contractor-side signal).
              * Conduit / earmarked-contribution disambiguation. A
                donor name may appear via ActBlue or similar; we
                count the transaction at face value. The "money
                flows from LEIE through conduit to candidate"
                pattern is a future signal that joins this one
                with FEC Schedule B-A earmark records.
            Tests: 15 live-pg.
              Schema: 'donor' entity_kind accepted; unknown still
              rejected; end-to-end happy path with full schema
              validation; corporate-shape donor name (no comma)
              does not fire; no LEIE match -> 0 rows; multi-
              contribution aggregation including middle-name-collapse;
              multi-LEIE record collapse picks freshest hash;
              memo_cd=X excluded from sum; negative amounts clipped
              by positive-only SUM; all-refund donor dropped via
              aggregated_active filter; idempotency; cycle
              isolation (2024 doesn't touch 2020); percentile shape
              (1-of-10 -> 0.9); evidence URL well-formed; empty
              inputs return 0 cleanly.
            Total assets: 27. Total asset checks: 44.
[X] FRAUD-F5d candidate_funded_by_excluded_donors cross-source signal
            (shipped 2026-05-04). SIXTH cross-source signal in the
            fraud engine; LEIE-analogue of
            candidate_funded_by_nj_contractor_employees (057).
            Candidate-side projection of donor_on_leie (059): rolls
            flagged-donor contributions through fec_committee.cand_id
            to fec_candidate, surfacing candidates whose campaigns
            are funded by federally-excluded individuals.
            Migration 060 ships:
              derived.refresh_signal_candidate_funded_by_excluded_donors(cycle)
                -- Reads matched-donor set from L1 (cycle,

                raw.fec_contribution -> raw.fec_committee ->
                raw.fec_candidate, sums positive transaction_amt per
                (cand_id, cand_office, cand_office_st), percentile-
                ranks per (office, state) bucket via CUME_DIST.
                MUST run after refresh_signal_donor_on_leie for the

                (memo_cd != 'X', transaction_amt > 0) so the
                candidate-side total reconciles with the donor-side
                total -- a divergence between the two filters would
                confuse the analyst.
                severity=5 CRITICAL. ESCALATED VS 057's severity=3:
                057 is a workforce-correlation signal (donor's
                employer overlaps a federal contractor); 060 is a
                direct procurement-influence signal (donor IS on
                the federal exclusion list).
                evidence_url shape:
                  /fec/risk/entities/candidate/<cand_id>
                    ?signal=candidate_funded_by_excluded_donors
                    &cycle=<cycle>

              derived.v_entity_fraud_risk gains

              Asset check match_rate_in_plausible_range (WARN):
                Standard cross-source candidate-side semantics
                (mirrors 057's check). Zero matches when both
                upstreams are non-empty -> regression (donor-side
                signal regressed, fec_committee.cand_id linkage
                broke, or canonicalizer drift). Match rate >50%
                of active-committee-tied candidate cohort ->
                upstream over-matching in 059.
            Deliberately out of scope (documented in migration 060
            header for future-session traceability):
              * JFC / Leadership PAC unrolling (requires
                raw.fec_ccl ingestion). Same gap as 057.
              * Independent expenditures (Super PAC spending FOR

              * Time-of-contribution proximity to procurement votes.
              * Multi-cycle aggregation -- a downstream view will
                consume this signal.

              End-to-end happy path with severity=5 assertion;
              zero-result when 059 not yet refreshed (regression
              tripwire for parallel-path refactors); non-candidate
              committees (cand_id NULL) drop out; multi-committee
              per candidate aggregates correctly; memo_cd=X
              filtered (mirrors 059); negative amounts filtered;

[X] DETECTION-QUALITY SLICE A (shipped 2026-05-04 same session).
            Per-signal min_actionable_threshold + multi-family
            diversity bonus. Rationale: Slice A is the highest-
            leverage detection-quality work because it directly
            improves the analyst-queue signal-to-noise ratio at
            two pinch points the prior architecture did not
            address.
            Migration 061 ships:
              - derived.fraud_signal_config(signal_id PK,
                signal_family, min_actionable_threshold, comment,
                created_at, updated_at) static config registry,
                seeded with all 14 known signals (4 leie_bearing,
                2 workforce, 1 address, 7 structural).
              - L2 view (v_entity_fraud_features) now INNER-JOINs
                the registry and applies the per-signal raw_value
                floor; below-threshold matches stay in L1
                (substrate honesty preserved) but drop out of
                L2/L3a/the analyst queue.
              - L2 view exposes a parallel signal_families[]
                column aligned with severities/percentiles.
              - 3-arg derived.fraud_risk_score(severities,
                percentiles, families). Same tail-only base
                (phi = sev*max(0,p-0.95)^2) plus a family-
                diversity term: bonus = 0.01 *
                max(0, n_contributing_families - 1)^2. Only
                families with at least one signal above the
                0.95 percentile threshold count, so noise
                families do not inflate the bonus.
              - L3a v_entity_fraud_risk rewired to call the
                3-arg fn; signal_families exposed downstream.
              - updated_at trigger on the config table for
                operator-edit audit traceability.
              - 14 seed rows: leie_bearing thresholds at $200
                (donor_on_leie, candidate_funded_by_excluded_donors)
                or $10K (entity_funded_and_excluded) or $0
                (entity_on_leie, binary); workforce thresholds at
                $1K (both); address/structural at $0 (binary).
            Dagster: derived.fraud_signal_config registered as a
            fingerprint asset (no upstream; static registry).
            v_entity_fraud_risk gains a dep edge on it so an
            operator UPDATE on a threshold propagates to the L3a
            fingerprint.
            Asset checks (both ERROR severity):
              every_signal_id_in_l1_has_a_config_row -- orphan-
                block: a signal_id in L1 without a config row
                INNER-JOINs out and silently disappears from the
                analyst queue. ERROR-severity fails the pipeline
                because this is a configuration bug not an
                environmental drift.
              multiple_families_present -- defends against an
                operator UPDATE that mass-rewrites the family
                column (which would neuter the diversity bonus).
            Tests: 19 live-pg.
              Schema: 4 CHECK constraints (unknown family,
              negative threshold, empty comment, invalid
              signal_id regex), updated_at trigger fires on
              UPDATE not INSERT, all 14 seeded signals have
              correct (family, threshold).
              L2 threshold filter: below-threshold L1 row drops
              from L2 but stays in L1; above-threshold L1 row is
              preserved; per-signal-not-per-entity filtering
              (entity firing on 2 signals where one is below
              threshold gets only the above-threshold signal in
              L2).
              signal_families column: parallel-array invariant
              with signal_ids in alphabetical sort order across
              3 different families.
              3-arg score function: NULL/empty -> 0; mismatched
              array lengths raise; single-signal one-family ~33;
              two-signals-one-family ~55 (no bonus); two-
              signals-two-families ~73 (with bonus); 3-fam > 2-
              fam at equal phi; below-0.95 signal does NOT count
              as contributing family for the bonus.
              End-to-end via L3a view: multi-family entity
              outscores single-family entity at equal phi.
            Pre-existing tests touched (latent-bug exposure):
              test_l2_pivot_aggregates_two_signals_into_one_row
              referenced a non-existent signal_id 'address_clusters'
              (real id is 'committee_address_clusters'); the
              migration's strict orphan-blocking surfaced this
              latent bug and the test was updated.
              test_live_view_exposes_score_per_entity expected
              score for entity Y bumped from 95.61 -> 97.34
              because Y's 5 signals span 2 families (4 structural
              + 1 address) and earn the +0.01 diversity term.
            Total assets: 29. Total asset checks: 47.

[X] DETECTION-QUALITY SLICE B: LEIE-age decay (shipped same
            session, 2026-05-04). LEIE-bearing signals now
            multiply raw_value by exp(-age_years/10) so older
            exclusions decay in actionable weight. Half-life
            ~6.93 years.
            Migration 062 ships:
              derived.f_leie_age_decay(p_excldate DATE) RETURNS
                NUMERIC. STABLE PARALLEL SAFE. NULL or future
                excldate -> 1.0 (conservative: no penalty for
                missing or anomalous data). tau = 10 was chosen
                by inspection of the LEIE enforcement vintage
                distribution -- about half of HHS-OIG exclusions
                are within the last 10 years, so a half-life
                around 7 years preserves the bulk of the
                active-enforcement era at >50% weight while
                aggressively decaying pre-2010 entries.
              054 entity_on_leie: raw_value 1.0 -> decay weight
                in (0, 1]. Rate-based percentile unchanged.
              058 entity_funded_and_excluded: SUM(award_amount)
                * decay(freshest_excldate). One decay call per
                matched person; equivalent to per-award decay
                since all matched awards share the same person's
                freshest exclusion.
              059 donor_on_leie: SUM(positive transaction_amt)
                * decay(freshest_excldate). Same per-person
                pattern as 058.
              060 candidate_funded_by_excluded_donors: PER-
                CONTRIBUTION decay because each candidate
                receives money from many distinct excluded
                donors with potentially different excldates. A
                new matched_donors_with_excldate CTE re-joins
                LEIE inside 060 (mirroring 059's
                leie_canonical_freshest CTE), then SUM(
                transaction_amt * f_leie_age_decay(excldate))
                per candidate.
            INTERACTION with 061's min_actionable_threshold:
              this is the design intent. A 12-year-old $20K
              contract decays to ~$6024 (below the $10K floor
              for entity_funded_and_excluded) and drops out of
              the analyst queue. Combining a magnitude floor
              with a recency decay surfaces only "currently-
              actionable" matches. Substrate-honesty preserved:
              the L1 row is still written; only the L2 view
              filters it out.
            Tests: 12 new live-pg in test_fraud_leie_age_decay.py.
              Decay shape: today=1.0, NULL=1.0, future-clamped
              to 1.0, 5y~exp(-0.5), 10y~exp(-1.0), 20y~exp(-2.0),
              monotone decreasing across (1, 5, 10, 20, 30) y.
              058 old-excldate raw_value scales correctly; 058
              decay-pushes-below-threshold validates substrate-
              honesty + L2 drop-out semantics; 059 5y decay
              validates per-donor scaling; 060 per-contribution
              decay validates the candidate roll-up's mixed-
              age donor handling (1 old + 1 new donor at $1K
              each -> ~$1368, not $2000); 054 binary -> decayed
              binary in (0, 1).
            Pre-existing test changes:
              13 LEIE-bearing tests in 058/059/060 that asserted
              literal-sum raw_value were preserved by changing
              _seed_leie_individual default excldate from
              "20200115" (~6y old, decay ~0.55) to "today"
              (decay 1.0). Tests that explicitly need an old
              excldate (e.g. multi-LEIE freshness collapse) now
              use the OLDER fixture as a probe and the NEWER
              fixture as today; the test assertion that
              raw_value equals the literal sum implicitly
              verifies the freshest LEIE record is selected
              (because picking the older one would decay the
              sum below it).
            Total assets: 29. Total asset checks: 47.

            NEXT (separate session, in priority order):
              1. FRAUD-F2 SAM.gov LOADER + cross-source signal
                 (deferred from this session). Substrate
                 (migration 063) is shipped; the loader reads

                 bump last_seen_at) and the cross-source signal
                 derived.refresh_signal_entity_excluded_via_sam_uei
                 joins USAspending.recipient_uei =
                 derived.v_sam_exclusion_by_uei.sam_uei

            and treasurer_is_candidate on the same row.

            DELIBERATE LIMITATIONS (substrate-honesty):
              * Name-only matching (no state/DOB filter). Common names
                produce false positives by design; severity=5 routes
                them all to analyst review. State filtering arrives in
                FRAUD-F5c when L5 labels exist to calibrate the recall
                cost.
              * No donor matches (raw.fec_contribution ~5M rows;
                donor_cluster entity_kind reserved but not yet plumbed).
              * No business / committee-name matches (LEIE BUSNAME is
                healthcare-org-shaped, FEC committee names are
                political-org-shaped; cross-source for businesses
                arrives with USAspending + SAM.gov in a future session).
              * No phonetic / trigram fuzzy matching (would require
                pg_trgm extension, deferred per AGENTS.md
                $0/Oracle-portability constraint).
